  Quarry: The enemy across becomes its quarry. This attacks it whenever it can.
  Take: When a unit's quarry dies, that unit makes a Take.

  Rainbow Chaser: Counts all seven slots, empty or occupied.

KEYWORDS:
  Mire: Start of Combat: the enemy across must attack this while this lives. This waits for it, once.
  Mired: Must attack the unit that mired it.
  Behind: Each back slot is behind two front slots: B1 behind F1 and F2.
  Quarry: The enemy across becomes its quarry. This attacks it whenever it can.
  Mark: Start of Combat: enemies that later enter the slot across take this much damage.

  Belly: Holds this many units, safe inside. When this dies, they come out.
  Ring: Whoever stands on this slot gains your Ring size for each Ring here.

  Chiffchaff: Played this turn, this sells normally. A homecoming doubles permanent stats, then returns this to hand.

  Cycle: One pass through a side's attack order.
  Mark: Start of Combat: enemies that later enter the slot across take this much damage.
  Bitter: When an enemy attacks this, it first loses this much Attack, down to 1.

  Tom Tit: Chosen at Start of Combat. The protection ends when Tom Tit dies.

KEYWORDS:
  Pack: When a unit beside this attacks, this joins in, using up its own attack.
  Beside: The slots left and right in the same row.
  Cycle: One pass through a side's attack order.
  Quarry: The enemy across becomes its quarry. This attacks it whenever it can.
  Take: When a unit's quarry dies, that unit makes a Take.
  Mire: Start of Combat: the enemy across must attack this while this lives. This waits for it, once.
  Bitter: When an enemy attacks this, it first loses this much Attack, down to 1.
  Ring: Whoever stands on this slot gains your Ring size for each Ring here.
  Belly: Holds this many units, safe inside. When this dies, they come out.
  Damage Reduction: Takes this much less damage from each hit.
  Fly South: Sold, it flies south, then comes home to hand with double stats.
  Egg: A 0/1 with no text that fights on an empty Nest.
  Mired: Must attack the unit that mired it.
  Mark: Start of Combat: enemies that later enter the slot across take this much damage.
DETAILS:

KEYWORDS:
  Ring: Whoever stands on this slot gains your Ring size for each Ring here.
  Belly: Holds this many units, safe inside. When this dies, they come out.
  Behind: Each back slot is behind two front slots: B1 behind F1 and F2.
  Quarry: The enemy across becomes its quarry. This attacks it whenever it can.
  Pounce: After a unit next to this has its quarry hit, this attacks it too. Once a cycle.
  Next to: Beside, in front or behind.
  Cycle: One pass through a side's attack order.
  Beside: The slots left and right in the same row.
  Fly South: Sold, it flies south, then comes home to hand with double stats.
  Acorn: Play for gold: Acorn 1, Stash 5, Great Hoard 16. Three make the next size.
  Damage Reduction: Takes this much less damage from each hit.
  Mark: Start of Combat: enemies that later enter the slot across take this much damage.
  Pack: When a unit beside this attacks, this joins in, using up its own attack.
  *: Golden: made from three copies, with stronger stats and text.
  Nightshade: When attacked, this first loses half its Health.
  Take: When a unit's quarry dies, that unit makes a Take.
  Egg: A 0/1 with no text that fights on an empty Nest.
DETAILS:
  Hedge Witch: Uses this slot's total Ring Attack, down to 0 Attack. A Fetch copying this reads its own slot.
  Figgy Duff: The golden goes to your hand and gives a Triple Reward when played.
  Cradle Fetch: Rounds copied stats up and adds this slot's Ring. Copies the biggest Fairy behind; two Fetches never share a target.
  Old Brock: A unit that already has a quarry keeps it.
  One Swallow: Trips last 1 turn. Played this turn, this sells normally; flying pays no sale gold. It doubles its permanent stats once per homecoming.
  The Cuckoo: Other Birds means Birds on your board, except the returning Bird. Fractions round up; copies trigger separately.
  Chiffchaff: Played this turn, this sells normally. A homecoming doubles permanent stats, then returns this to hand.
  Mrs Jay: Flying counts as selling. Played this turn, this sells normally.
  Tom Tit: Chosen at Start of Combat. The protection ends when Tom Tit dies.
  Mr Redbreast: Reads its permanent stats after homecoming growth. Each fractional stat rounds up; copies trigger separately.
  Young Brock: A unit that already has a quarry keeps it, without the Attack.
  The Toll-Bard: Attack loss twice a fight; gold once a fight.
  Lady Belladonna: Beside means adjacent in the same row. Nightshade is Health loss, so Shield does not stop it.
  Redwing: Uses the Birds' permanent stats, rounded up. Only while Redwing lives; multiple copies use the strongest aura.
  Old Tern: Only Birds that have missed a fight can be recalled early. Each homecoming doubles once; it returns to hand.
  Mag Pye: Resolves before other Start of Combat text. With no friendly Bird, this does nothing; next to includes in front and behind.

  Belly: Holds this many units, safe inside. When this dies, they come out.
  Ring size: What each Ring gives; starts at +1/+1.
  Fly South: Sold, it flies south, then comes home to hand with double stats.
  Damage Reduction: Takes this much less damage from each hit.
  Mark: Start of Combat: enemies that later enter the slot across take this much damage.
  Pack: When a unit beside this attacks, this joins in, using up its own attack.
  *: Golden: made from three copies, with stronger stats and text.
  Nightshade: When attacked, this first loses half its Health.
  Take: When a unit's quarry dies, that unit makes a Take.
  Acorn: Play for gold: Acorn 1, Stash 5, Great Hoard 16. Three make the next size.
  Egg: A 0/1 with no text that fights on an empty Nest.
DETAILS:
  Reel-Mistress: Next means your next attacker. Each Ring keeps its move count when Rings meet; Rings return to their slots after combat.
  Old Brock: A unit that already has a quarry keeps it.
  Elder Ooze: The buff applies while this lives, and to its own Belly’s units when this dies. Drips count. Only your own units gain it, never returned enemies.
  Hasty Pudding: It goes back when this dies, after 3 of your units die, or when its side has nobody else.
  Cluricaun: Empty slots count. Copies grow once per tier-up, using the strongest copy; a golden copy grows twice as much.
  The Cuckoo: Other Birds means Birds on your board, except the returning Bird. Fractions round up; copies trigger separately.
  Tom Tit: Chosen at Start of Combat. The protection ends when Tom Tit dies.
  Mr Redbreast: Reads its permanent stats after homecoming growth. Each fractional stat rounds up; copies trigger separately.
  Young Brock: A unit that already has a quarry keeps it, without the Attack.
  The Toll-Bard: Attack loss twice a fight; gold once a fight.
  Lady Belladonna: Beside means adjacent in the same row. Nightshade is Health loss, so Shield does not stop it.
  One Swallow: Trips last 1 turn. Played this turn, this sells normally; flying pays no sale gold. It doubles its permanent stats once per homecoming.
  Chiffchaff: Played this turn, this sells normally. A homecoming doubles permanent stats, then returns this to hand.
  Mrs Jay: Flying counts as selling. Played this turn, this sells normally.
  Redwing: Uses the Birds' permanent stats, rounded up. Only while Redwing lives; multiple copies use the strongest aura.
  Old Tern: Only Birds that have missed a fight can be recalled early. Each homecoming doubles once; it returns to hand.
  Mag Pye: Resolves before other Start of Combat text. With no friendly Bird, this does nothing; next to includes in front and behind.

KEYWORDS:
  Beside: The slots left and right in the same row.
  Mire: Start of Combat: the enemy across must attack this while this lives. This waits for it, once.
  Bitter: When an enemy attacks this, it first loses this much Attack, down to 1.
  Fly South: Sold, it flies south, then comes home to hand with double stats.
  Damage Reduction: Takes this much less damage from each hit.
  Acorn: Play for gold: Acorn 1, Stash 5, Great Hoard 16. Three make the next size.
  Cleave: Attacks also hit the units beside the target.
  Nest: The slot a Bird left; a 0/1 Egg fights here unless a unit stands here.
  Belly: Holds this many units, safe inside. When this dies, they come out.
  Pack: When a unit beside this attacks, this joins in, using up its own attack.
  *: Golden: made from three copies, with stronger stats and text.
  Quarry: The enemy across becomes its quarry. This attacks it whenever it can.
  Mark: Start of Combat: enemies that later enter the slot across take this much damage.
  Pounce: After a unit next to this has its quarry hit, this attacks it too. Once a cycle.
  Next to: Beside, in front or behind.
  Cycle: One pass through a side's attack order.
  Behind: Each back slot is behind two front slots: B1 behind F1 and F2.
  Nightshade: When attacked, this first loses half its Health.
DETAILS:
  Pipistrelle Echo: Repeats spend no gold, so Last Orders repeats give no stats.
  Changeling: This takes its place on their side; then both go home. Each unit uses its new slot's Ring.
  The Prima Donna: Once per enemy is spent when the attack is declared, even if no damage lands.
  Chiffchaff: Played this turn, this sells normally. A homecoming doubles permanent stats, then returns this to hand.
  The Cuckoo: Other Birds means Birds on your board, except the returning Bird. Fractions round up; copies trigger separately.
  One Swallow: Trips last 1 turn. Played this turn, this sells normally; flying pays no sale gold. It doubles its permanent stats once per homecoming.
  Tom Tit: Chosen at Start of Combat. The protection ends when Tom Tit dies.
  Mr Redbreast: Reads its permanent stats after homecoming growth. Each fractional stat rounds up; copies trigger separately.
  Mrs Jay: Flying counts as selling. Played this turn, this sells normally.
  The Great Lump: Borrows permanent stats while that Bird is away. The shop shows the borrowed stats; they disappear when the Bird comes home.
  Bubble and Squeak: The copy appears in the nearest gap with an empty Belly.
  Mrs Stork: The count carries between turns. Calls the eligible Bird away longest; ties choose the biggest, then reading order.
  Jack Daw: Fractions round up. The enemy's loss ends with the fight; your Bird keeps its gain.
  Old Tern: Only Birds that have missed a fight can be recalled early. Each homecoming doubles once; it returns to hand.
  Young Brock: A unit that already has a quarry keeps it, without the Attack.
  The Toll-Bard: Attack loss twice a fight; gold once a fight.
  Old Brock: A unit that already has a quarry keeps it.
  Lady Belladonna: Beside means adjacent in the same row. Nightshade is Health loss, so Shield does not stop it.

Hero: Brother Bracken. Start of Combat: with 5 or fewer units, they gain +1/+1 per tier, up to +5/+5.

SHOP (buy N):
  0 Pipistrelle Echo 3/6 T5 neutral (3 gold): Spells you aim at this also hit the units beside it.
  1 Stone Soup 1/3 T2 Oozes (3 gold): Belly 1. After you put a unit into any Belly: gain +1/+1 permanently. Once a turn.
  2 Lady Belladonna 4/8 T5 Fairies (3 gold): Start of Combat: give the enemy across Nightshade.
  3 The Peat King 5/9 T6 Bog folk (3 gold): Mire. After a Mired enemy dies: summon a 4/4 Peatling with Bitter 2. Twice a fight.
  4 Will-o'-the-Wisp 3/6 T4 Fairies (3 gold): Start of Combat: summon a 1/1 Wisp into each empty Ring.
  5 The Gabble Ratchet 6/10 T6 Birds (3 gold): When a unit on a Nest dies: its Bird drops in.
  6 Hiring Fair (spell T5, 6 gold): Discover a tier 6 unit. If 5 of your units share a tribe, one option is that tribe.
BOARD:
  F1 Mother Goose 15/19 T5 Birds: Fly South. When played: get a Golden Egg. [sell: 0 gold] [sell: flies south] {Nest}

  F3 One Swallow 36/54 T1 Birds: Fly South. When played: get 1 gold. [sell: 0 gold] [sell: flies south] [Slot: Ring +1/+1]
  F4 One Swallow 23/29 T1 Birds: Fly South. When played: get 1 gold. [sell: 0 gold] [sell: flies south]

HAND 2/10 (play @handle [slot]):
  @51 (number 0) Chiffchaff 57/73 T1 Birds: Fly South. When played: gain +1/+1.
  @828 (number 1) Mrs Jay 17/17 T2 Birds: Fly South. When sold: get an Acorn.
KEYWORDS:
  Beside: The slots left and right in the same row.
  Belly: Holds this many units, safe inside. When this dies, they come out.
  Nightshade: When attacked, this first loses half its Health.
  Mire: Start of Combat: the enemy across must attack this while this lives. This waits for it, once.
  Bitter: When an enemy attacks this, it first loses this much Attack, down to 1.
  Mired: Must attack the unit that mired it.
  Ring: Whoever stands on this slot gains your Ring size for each Ring here.
  Nest: The slot a Bird left; a 0/1 Egg fights here unless a unit stands here.
  Fly South: Sold, it flies south, then comes home to hand with double stats.
  Egg: A 0/1 with no text that fights on an empty Nest.
  Cleave: Attacks also hit the units beside the target.
  Acorn: Play for gold: Acorn 1, Stash 5, Great Hoard 16. Three make the next size.
  Pack: When a unit beside this attacks, this joins in, using up its own attack.
  Quarry: The enemy across becomes its quarry. This attacks it whenever it can.
  Take: When a unit's quarry dies, that unit makes a Take.
  Pounce: After a unit next to this has its quarry hit, this attacks it too. Once a cycle.
  Next to: Beside, in front or behind.
  Cycle: One pass through a side's attack order.
  Behind: Each back slot is behind two front slots: B1 behind F1 and F2.
  *: Golden: made from three copies, with stronger stats and text.
  Shy: Only a spell aimed at this buffs it. Then it leaves: get 2 gold next turn.
  Shield: Prevents the first damage this would take each fight.
DETAILS:
  Pipistrelle Echo: Repeats spend no gold, so Last Orders repeats give no stats.
  Stone Soup: Recruit Phase only. Shop, hand and board units count, including its own Belly.
  Lady Belladonna: Beside means adjacent in the same row. Nightshade is Health loss, so Shield does not stop it.
  The Peat King: The Peatling appears in your empty slot nearest this.
  Will-o'-the-Wisp: Wisps gain their slots' Rings. They exist only for the fight.
  The Gabble Ratchet: Once per Nest a fight. The Bird stays away afterwards with its trip unchanged; this is not a homecoming.
  Mother Goose: A Golden Egg doubles one Bird permanently, once per Bird. A golden Bird comes home at the same multiplier as any Bird.
  The Cuckoo: Other Birds means Birds on your board, except the returning Bird. Fractions round up; copies trigger separately.
  One Swallow: Trips last 1 turn. Played this turn, this sells normally; flying pays no sale gold. It doubles its permanent stats once per homecoming.
  The Great Lump: Borrows permanent stats while that Bird is away. The shop shows the borrowed stats; they disappear when the Bird comes home.
  Mr Redbreast: Reads its permanent stats after homecoming growth. Each fractional stat rounds up; copies trigger separately.
  Chiffchaff: Played this turn, this sells normally. A homecoming doubles permanent stats, then returns this to hand.
  Mrs Jay: Flying counts as selling. Played this turn, this sells normally.
  Mrs Stork: The count carries between turns. Calls the eligible Bird away longest; ties choose the biggest, then reading order.
  The Toll-Bard: Attack loss twice a fight; gold once a fight.
  Old Brock: A unit that already has a quarry keeps it.
  Bubble and Squeak: The copy appears in the nearest gap with an empty Belly.
  Figgy Duff: The golden goes to your hand and gives a Triple Reward when played.
  Hasty Pudding: It goes back when this dies, after 3 of your units die, or when its side has nobody else.
  Hob-in-the-Hearth: Shy pays 2 gold; chooses after Cradle Fetch copies. Shield prevents damage; Nightshade is Health loss.

  Pack: When a unit beside this attacks, this joins in, using up its own attack.

KEYWORDS:
  Belly: Holds this many units, safe inside. When this dies, they come out.
  Take: When a unit's quarry dies, that unit makes a Take.
  Quarry: The enemy across becomes its quarry. This attacks it whenever it can.
  Pack: When a unit beside this attacks, this joins in, using up its own attack.
  Beside: The slots left and right in the same row.
  Snap: Attacks the Mired enemy with the most Attack.
  Mired: Must attack the unit that mired it.
  Fly South: Sold, it flies south, then comes home to hand with double stats.
  Acorn: Play for gold: Acorn 1, Stash 5, Great Hoard 16. Three make the next size.
  Cleave: Attacks also hit the units beside the target.
  Nest: The slot a Bird left; a 0/1 Egg fights here unless a unit stands here.
  *: Golden: made from three copies, with stronger stats and text.
  Ring: Whoever stands on this slot gains your Ring size for each Ring here.
  Behind: Each back slot is behind two front slots: B1 behind F1 and F2.
  Pounce: After a unit next to this has its quarry hit, this attacks it too. Once a cycle.
  Next to: Beside, in front or behind.
  Cycle: One pass through a side's attack order.
  Egg: A 0/1 with no text that fights on an empty Nest.
DETAILS:
  Elder Ooze: The buff applies while this lives, and to its own Belly’s units when this dies. Drips count. Only your own units gain it, never returned enemies.
  The Laird of the Sett: A unit that already has a quarry keeps it.
  Old Isegrim: The bonus applies only to each joiner’s damage in that attack.
  The Lantern Man: The Attack bonus changes as enemies enter and leave Mire.
  Changeling: This takes its place on their side; then both go home. Each unit uses its new slot's Ring.
  Mrs Jay: Flying counts as selling. Played this turn, this sells normally.
  The Cuckoo: Other Birds means Birds on your board, except the returning Bird. Fractions round up; copies trigger separately.
  The Great Lump: Borrows permanent stats while that Bird is away. The shop shows the borrowed stats; they disappear when the Bird comes home.
  The Seven Whistlers: Counts different Bird card names on your board. Each stat rounds up after halving; Eggs do not count.
  The Gabble Ratchet: Once per Nest a fight. The Bird stays away afterwards with its trip unchanged; this is not a homecoming.
  Mr Redbreast: Reads its permanent stats after homecoming growth. Each fractional stat rounds up; copies trigger separately.
  Chiffchaff: Played this turn, this sells normally. A homecoming doubles permanent stats, then returns this to hand.
  One Swallow: Trips last 1 turn. Played this turn, this sells normally; flying pays no sale gold. It doubles its permanent stats once per homecoming.
  Queen of Elfland: Destroyed Shy units pay no gold and do not count as leaving. With no unit behind, this does nothing.
  Mrs Stork: The count carries between turns. Calls the eligible Bird away longest; ties choose the biggest, then reading order.
  The Toll-Bard: Attack loss twice a fight; gold once a fight.
  Old Brock: A unit that already has a quarry keeps it.
  Mother Goose: A Golden Egg doubles one Bird permanently, once per Bird. A golden Bird comes home at the same multiplier as any Bird.

KEYWORDS:
  Ring: Whoever stands on this slot gains your Ring size for each Ring here.
  Belly: Holds this many units, safe inside. When this dies, they come out.
  Base: A card's own stats, without any buffs.
  Mire: Start of Combat: the enemy across must attack this while this lives. This waits for it, once.
  Bitter: When an enemy attacks this, it first loses this much Attack, down to 1.
  Beside: The slots left and right in the same row.
  Fly South: Sold, it flies south, then comes home to hand with double stats.
  Acorn: Play for gold: Acorn 1, Stash 5, Great Hoard 16. Three make the next size.
  Cleave: Attacks also hit the units beside the target.
  Nest: The slot a Bird left; a 0/1 Egg fights here unless a unit stands here.

DETAILS:

  The Prima Donna: Once per enemy is spent when the attack is declared, even if no damage lands.
  Mrs Jay: Flying counts as selling. Played this turn, this sells normally.
  The Cuckoo: Other Birds means Birds on your board, except the returning Bird. Fractions round up; copies trigger separately.

  Mr Redbreast: Reads its permanent stats after homecoming growth. Each fractional stat rounds up; copies trigger separately.
  The Great Lump: Borrows permanent stats while that Bird is away. The shop shows the borrowed stats; they disappear when the Bird comes home.
  Mother Goose: A Golden Egg doubles one Bird permanently, once per Bird. A golden Bird comes home at the same multiplier as any Bird.
  Redwing: Uses the Birds' permanent stats, rounded up. Only while Redwing lives; multiple copies use the strongest aura.
  Mag Pye: Resolves before other Start of Combat text. With no friendly Bird, this does nothing; next to includes in front and behind.
  The Gabble Ratchet: Once per Nest a fight. The Bird stays away afterwards with its trip unchanged; this is not a homecoming.
  The Seven Whistlers: Counts different Bird card names on your board. Each stat rounds up after halving; Eggs do not count.
  Queen of Elfland: Destroyed Shy units pay no gold and do not count as leaving. With no unit behind, this does nothing.

Hero: Brother Bracken. Start of Combat: with 5 or fewer units, they gain +1/+1 per tier, up to +5/+5.
Last Stand: used.
SHOP (buy N):
  0 The Lodger 4/4 T3 Hunters (3 gold): Pounce. After this pounces: gain +2/+1.
  1 Fairy Monarch 6/8 T6 Fairies (3 gold): Every 3rd End of Turn: double your Ring size.
  2 Charcoal Burner 4/6 T4 neutral (3 gold): End of Turn: burn up to 6 of your gold above 5. Units behind this gain +1/+1 per 2 burned.
  3 Old Isegrim 6/8 T6 Hunters (3 gold): Pack. Your units have +3 Attack while joining an attack.
  4 The Peat King 5/9 T6 Bog folk (3 gold): Mire. After a Mired enemy dies: summon a 4/4 Peatling with Bitter 2. Twice a fight.
  5 Old Isegrim 6/8 T6 Hunters (3 gold): Pack. Your units have +3 Attack while joining an attack.
  6 Harvest Supper (spell T3, 3 gold, aim tribe_unit): Pick a unit. Your units of its tribe, now and later, gain +1/+1, up to +3/+3. Once a turn.
BOARD:
  F1 (empty) {Nest}
  F2 The Cuckoo 53/69 T4 Birds: After a Bird comes home: other Birds gain a tenth of its stats. [sell: 1 gold]
  F3 Mother Goose 30/38 T5 Birds: Fly South. When played: get a Golden Egg. [sell: 1 gold] [sell: gone, played this turn] [Slot: Ring +1/+1] {Nest}
  F4 The Gabble Ratchet 7/11 T6 Birds: When a unit on a Nest dies: its Bird drops in. [sell: 1 gold] {Nest}
  B1 The Seven Whistlers 9/11 T6 Birds: Start of Combat: halve the enemy across. With seven different Birds, halve every enemy. [sell: 1 gold] [Slot: Ring +1/+1] {Nest}
  B2 Mr Redbreast 148/191 T2 Birds: After a Bird comes home: gain an eighth of its stats. [sell: 1 gold]

HAND 0/10 (play @handle [slot]):
KEYWORDS:
  Pounce: After a unit next to this has its quarry hit, this attacks it too. Once a cycle.
  Next to: Beside, in front or behind.
  Cycle: One pass through a side's attack order.
  Ring size: What each Ring gives; starts at +1/+1.
  Ring: Whoever stands on this slot gains your Ring size for each Ring here.
  Behind: Each back slot is behind two front slots: B1 behind F1 and F2.
  Pack: When a unit beside this attacks, this joins in, using up its own attack.
  Beside: The slots left and right in the same row.
  Mire: Start of Combat: the enemy across must attack this while this lives. This waits for it, once.
  Bitter: When an enemy attacks this, it first loses this much Attack, down to 1.
  Mired: Must attack the unit that mired it.
  Fly South: Sold, it flies south, then comes home to hand with double stats.
  Egg: A 0/1 with no text that fights on an empty Nest.
  Nest: The slot a Bird left; a 0/1 Egg fights here unless a unit stands here.
  Cleave: Attacks also hit the units beside the target.
  *: Golden: made from three copies, with stronger stats and text.
  Acorn: Play for gold: Acorn 1, Stash 5, Great Hoard 16. Three make the next size.
DETAILS:
  Fairy Monarch: Midsummer shares one countdown across copies. Extra triggers never advance it or repeat its growth; a golden copy triples instead.
  Old Isegrim: The bonus applies only to each joiner’s damage in that attack.
  The Peat King: The Peatling appears in your empty slot nearest this.
  The Cuckoo: Other Birds means Birds on your board, except the returning Bird. Fractions round up; copies trigger separately.
  Mother Goose: A Golden Egg doubles one Bird permanently, once per Bird. A golden Bird comes home at the same multiplier as any Bird.
  The Gabble Ratchet: Once per Nest a fight. The Bird stays away afterwards with its trip unchanged; this is not a homecoming.
  The Seven Whistlers: Counts different Bird card names on your board. Each stat rounds up after halving; Eggs do not count.
  Mr Redbreast: Reads its permanent stats after homecoming growth. Each fractional stat rounds up; copies trigger separately.
  The Great Lump: Borrows permanent stats while that Bird is away. The shop shows the borrowed stats; they disappear when the Bird comes home.
  Harvest Supper: Units summoned in fights don't get it.
  Redwing: Uses the Birds' permanent stats, rounded up. Only while Redwing lives; multiple copies use the strongest aura.
  Mag Pye: Resolves before other Start of Combat text. With no friendly Bird, this does nothing; next to includes in front and behind.
  Chiffchaff: Played this turn, this sells normally. A homecoming doubles permanent stats, then returns this to hand.
  Mrs Jay: Flying counts as selling. Played this turn, this sells normally.
  One Swallow: Trips last 1 turn. Played this turn, this sells normally; flying pays no sale gold. It doubles its permanent stats once per homecoming.
  Queen of Elfland: Destroyed Shy units pay no gold and do not count as leaving. With no unit behind, this does nothing.

  Mr Redbreast: Reads its permanent stats after homecoming growth. Each fractional stat rounds up; copies trigger separately.

  Harvest Supper: Units summoned in fights don't get it.
  The Seven Whistlers: Counts different Bird card names on your board. Each stat rounds up after halving; Eggs do not count.
  Chiffchaff: Played this turn, this sells normally. A homecoming doubles permanent stats, then returns this to hand.
  Silky: Shy pays 2 gold; only thanking counts, including Silky herself. Copies grow once per departure, using the strongest copy.
  Mrs Jay: Flying counts as selling. Played this turn, this sells normally.
